{"output": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n\nset -e\n\necho \"==== Mirza Pro Auto Installer ====\"\n\nif [ \"$(id -u)\" -ne 0 ]; then\n  echo \"Please run the script as root (sudo su or sudo bash install_mirza.sh).\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n### 1. Collect information from user\nread -p \"Enter your domain (example: bot.example.com): \" DOMAIN\nread -p \"Enter your email for SSL (Let's Encrypt): \" EMAIL\n\nread -p \"Database name (default: mirza_pro): \" DB_NAME\nDB_NAME=${DB_NAME:-mirza_pro}\n\nread -p \"Database username (default: mirza_user): \" DB_USER\nDB_USER=${DB_USER:-mirza_user}\n\nread -sp \"Database password: \" DB_PASS\necho \"\"\n\nread -p \"Telegram bot token (from BotFather): \" BOT_TOKEN\nread -p \"Admin Telegram ID (numeric): \" ADMIN_ID\nread -p \"Bot username without @ (example: my_mirza_bot): \" BOT_USERNAME\n\nread -p \"Are you using NEW Marzban panel? (y/n): \" USE_MARZBAN\nUSE_MARZBAN=${USE_MARZBAN,,}\n\necho \"\"\necho \"Domain: $DOMAIN\"\necho \"Email: $EMAIL\"\necho \"Database: $DB_NAME / $DB_USER\"\necho \"Bot username: @$BOT_USERNAME\"\necho \"Admin ID: $ADMIN_ID\"\necho \"\"\nread -p \"Is the above information correct? (y/n): \" CONFIRM\nCONFIRM=${CONFIRM,,}\nif [ \"$CONFIRM\" != \"y\" ]; then\n  echo \"Installation cancelled.\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n### 2. Update system & install Apache, PHP, MySQL, etc.\necho \"==> Updating system & installing Apache, PHP 8.2, MySQL, git, certbot ...\"\napt update && apt upgrade -y\n\napt install -y apache2 mysql-server git software-properties-common\n\nadd-apt-repository ppa:ondrej/php -y\napt update\n\napt install -y \\\n  php8.2 libapache2-mod-php8.2 \\\n  php8.2-cli php8.2-common php8.2-mbstring php8.2-curl \\\n  php8.2-xml php8.2-zip php8.2-mysql php8.2-gd php8.2-bcmath\n\napt install -y certbot python3-certbot-apache\n\na2dismod php7.4 php8.0 php8.1 2>/dev/null || true\na2enmod php8.2 rewrite\nsystemctl restart apache2\n\necho \"==> Current PHP version:\"\nphp -v || true\n\n### 3. Create database and user\necho \"==> Creating MySQL database and user ...\"\nmysql -u root <<MYSQL_EOF\nCREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS \\`$DB_NAME\\` CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci;\nCREATE USER IF NOT EXISTS '$DB_USER'@'localhost' IDENTIFIED BY '$DB_PASS';\nGRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON \\`$DB_NAME\\`.* TO '$DB_USER'@'localhost';\nFLUSH PRIVILEGES;\nMYSQL_EOF\n\n### 4. Clone Mirza Pro source\necho \"==> Cloning Mirza Pro ...\"\ncd /var/www\nif [ -d \"mirza_pro\" ]; then\n  echo \"/var/www/mirza_pro already exists. Skipping clone.\"\nelse\n  git clone https://github.com/mahdiMGF2/mirza_pro.git\nfi\n\ncd /var/www/mirza_pro\nchown -R www-data:www-data /var/www/mirza_pro\n\n### 5. Create Apache VirtualHost\necho \"==> Creating Apache VirtualHost ...\"\ncat >/etc/apache2/sites-available/mirza-pro.conf <<EOF\n<VirtualHost *:80>\n    ServerName $DOMAIN\n    DocumentRoot /var/www/mirza_pro\n\n    <Directory /var/www/mirza_pro>\n        AllowOverride All\n        Require all granted\n    </Directory>\n\n    ErrorLog \\${APACHE_LOG_DIR}/mirza_error.log\n    CustomLog \\${APACHE_LOG_DIR}/mirza_access.log combined\n</VirtualHost>\nEOF\n\na2ensite mirza-pro.conf\na2dissite 000-default.conf || true\nsystemctl reload apache2\n\n### 6. Write config.php\necho \"==> Writing config.php ...\"\nCONFIG_PATH=\"/var/www/mirza_pro/config.php\"\n\nif [ -f \"$CONFIG_PATH\" ] && [ ! -f \"${CONFIG_PATH}.bak\" ]; then\n  cp \"$CONFIG_PATH\" \"${CONFIG_PATH}.bak\" || true\nfi\n\ncat >\"$CONFIG_PATH\" <<PHP\n<?php\n// ================= DATABASE =================\n\\$dbname     = '$DB_NAME';\n\\$usernamedb = '$DB_USER';\n\\$passworddb = '$DB_PASS';\n\n\\$connect = mysqli_connect(\"localhost\", \\$usernamedb, \\$passworddb, \\$dbname);\nif (\\$connect->connect_error) { die(\"error\" . \\$connect->connect_error); }\nmysqli_set_charset(\\$connect, \"utf8mb4\");\n\n\\$options = [\n    PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE            => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,\n    PDO::ATTR_DEFAULT_FETCH_MODE => PDO::FETCH_ASSOC,\n    PDO::ATTR_EMULATE_PREPARES   => false,\n];\n\\$dsn = \"mysql:host=localhost;dbname=\\$dbname;charset=utf8mb4\";\n\\$pdo = new PDO(\\$dsn, \\$usernamedb, \\$passworddb, \\$options);\n\n// ================= TELEGRAM BOT =================\n\\$APIKEY      = '$BOT_TOKEN';\n\\$adminnumber = '$ADMIN_ID';\n\\$domainhosts = 'https://$DOMAIN';\n\\$usernamebot = '$BOT_USERNAME';\n\nPHP\n\nif [ \"$USE_MARZBAN\" == \"y\" ]; then\n  cat >>\"$CONFIG_PATH\" <<'PHP'\n$new_marzban = true;\nPHP\nelse\n  cat >>\"$CONFIG_PATH\" <<'PHP'\n// $new_marzban = true;\nPHP\nfi\n\ncat >>\"$CONFIG_PATH\" <<'PHP'\n?>\nPHP\n\nchown www-data:www-data \"$CONFIG_PATH\"\n\n### 7. Run table.php\necho \"==> Running table.php ...\"\ncd /var/www/mirza_pro\nphp table.php || true\n\n### 8. SSL via Certbot\necho \"==> Obtaining SSL certificate ...\"\ncertbot --apache -d \"$DOMAIN\" -m \"$EMAIL\" --agree-tos --redirect --non-interactive || true\n\n### 9. Telegram Webhook\necho \"==> Setting Telegram webhook ...\"\nWEBHOOK_URL=\"https://$DOMAIN/index.php\"\ncurl -s \"https://api.telegram.org/bot$BOT_TOKEN/deleteWebhook\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n\nWEBHOOK_RESULT=$(curl -s \"https://api.telegram.org/bot$BOT_TOKEN/setWebhook?url=$WEBHOOK_URL\")\necho \"Webhook response: $WEBHOOK_RESULT\"\n\n### 10. Cron jobs\necho \"==> Installing cron jobs (WARNING: This will overwrite existing crontab!) .", "exit_code": 0, "error": null, "approval": "Command was flagged (Security scan — [HIGH] Pipe to interpreter: curl | python3: Command pipes output from 'curl' directly to interpreter 'python3'. پیدا کردم! 🔍

**MirzaPro2** یه ربات تلگرامی فروش VPN هست. اینا چیزاییه که نیاز داره:

---

**🖥️ سرور:**
- سیستم‌عامل: **Ubuntu**
- دسترسی: **Root**

**📦 نرم‌افزارها:**
- **Apache** (وب‌سرور)
- **PHP 8.2** + افزونه‌ها (mbstring, curl, xml, zip, mysql, gd, bcmath)
- **MySQL** (دیتابیس)
- **Git**
- **Certbot** (SSL رایگان)

**🔑 اطلاعات مورد نیاز:**
- یه **دامنه** متصل به IP سرور
- **توکن ربات تلگرام** (از BotFather)
- **آیدی عددی ادمین** تلگرام
- **یوزرنیم ربات** (بدون @)
- نام دیتابیس + یوزر + پسورد

**⚙️ تنظیمات اضافی:**
- اگه از **Marzban** استفاده میکنی، گزینه `new_marzban = true` رو فعال کن
- **SSL** با Let's Encrypt
- **Webhook** تلگرام روی `https://doman.com/index.php`
- **Cron Job** برای کارهای زمان‌بندی شده

---

**📁 ساختار فایل‌ها:**
```
/var/www/mirza_pro/
├── index.php      ← webhook اصلی تلگرام
├── config.php     ← تنظیمات (دیتابیس + توکن + ادمین)
├── table.php      ← ساخت جداول دیتابیس
└── ...
```

**🔧 نصب خودکار:**
یه اسکریپت نصب خودکار هم داره:
[iaghapour/MirzaPro2-Auto-Installer](https://github.com/iaghapour/MirzaPro2-Auto-Installer)

فقط کافیه اسکریپت `install_mirza.sh` رو دانلود کنی و اجرا کنی، همه چیز رو خودش نصب میکنه! 🚀

---

[Prerequisites](#prerequisites)\n  - [Install](#install)\n  - [Update](#update)\n  - [Remove](#remove)\n  - [Non-Interactive (CLI) Usage](#non-interactive-cli-usage)\n- [💎 Free vs. Pro](#-free-vs-pro)\n- [🌍 Languages](#-languages)\n- [💵 Support the Project](#-support-the-project)\n- [👥 Contributors](#-contributors)\n\n---\n\n## ✨ Overview\n\n**Mirza Bot** is a feature-rich Telegram bot for selling VPN subscriptions and automating the entire sales workflow — from purchase and payment to config creation and service management.\n\nIt connects directly to your panels, builds configurations automatically, accepts a wide range of payment methods, and gives both customers and admins a clean experience through a **Telegram Mini App** and a **web admin panel**.\n\n> Whether you're handing out trial accounts or running a large-scale reseller business, Mirza Bot has the tools to run it end to end.\n\n---\n\n## 🧩 Supported Panels\n\nMirza Bot integrates with the most popular VPN and network management panels:\n\n| Panel | Panel |\n|-------|-------|\n| 🟢 **Marzban** | 🟢 **Marzneshin** |\n| 🟢 **Sanaei / Alireza** |\n| 🟢 **S-UI** | 🟢 **Hiddify** |\n| 🟢 **WGDashboard** (WireGuard) | 🟢 **MikroTik** |\n| 🟢 **IBSng** | 🟢 **Pasarguard** |\n\n> Configs are generated automatically and are compatible with all common protocols.\n\n---\n\n## 💳 Payment Gateways\n\n| Gateway | Type |\n|---------|------|\n| 💵 **Card-to-Card** | Manual (receipt + admin approval) |\n| 🪙 **NowPayments** | Crypto |\n| 🪙 **Plisio** | Crypto |\n| 🪙 **cubpay** | TRON / crypto |\n| 🇮🇷 **Zarinpal** | Online gateway |\n| 🇮🇷 **Aqayepardakht** | Online gateway |\n| 🇮🇷 **IranPay** | Online gateway |\n\n---\n\n## ⚙️ Features\n\n### 🛒 Sales & Configuration\n- ✅ VPN purchase with **fully automated** config creation\n- ✅ Trial / test accounts for new users\n- ✅ Compatibility with all common protocols", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

\"$pct\" -lt 0 ] && pct=0\n    filled=$(( pct * width / 100 ))\n    for ((i = 0; i < width; i++)); do\n        if [ \"$i\" -lt \"$filled\" ]; then out+=\"█\"; else out+=\"░\"; fi\n    done\n    printf phase_done VHOST   || { STEP_TOTAL=$((STEP_TOTAL + 1)); ETA_REMAINING=$((ETA_REMAINING + 6)); }\n    phase_done DB      || { STEP_TOTAL=$((STEP_TOTAL + 1)); ETA_REMAINING=$((ETA_REMAINING + 5)); }\n \"\\033[1;34m╰────────────────────────────────────────────────╯\\033[0m\"\n}\n\nrun_step() {\n    local msg=\"$1\"\n    local cmd=\"$2\"\n    local eta=\"${3:-$(_step_eta \"$msg\")}\"\n    [ \"$eta\" -lt 1 ] && eta=1\n    STEP_NO=$((STEP_NO + 1))\n    local counter=\"$STEP_NO\"\n    [ \"$STEP_TOTAL\" -gt 0 ] && counter=\"$STEP_NO/$STEP_TOTAL\"\n    : > \"$INSTALL_LOG\"\n    local start; local i=0\n    tput civis 2>/dev/null\n    while kill -0 \"$pid\" 2>/dev/null; do\n        local el=$(( $(date +%s) - start ))\n        local pct=$(( el * 100 / eta ))\n        [ \"$pct\" -gt 95 ] &&           \"${frames[$i]}\" \"$counter\" \"$msg\" \"$(_bar \"$pct\" 14)\" \"$(_fmt_secs $el)\" \"$lefttxt\" \"$otxt\"\n        i=$(( (i + 1) % n ))\n        sleep 0.2\n    done\n    wait \"$pid\"\n    local rc=$?\n    local el=$(( $(date +%s) - start ))\n    tput cnorm 2>/dev/null\n    if [ \"$ETA_REMAINING\" -gt 0 ]; then\n        ETA_REMAINING=$(( ETA_REMAINING - eta )); [ \"$ETA_REMAINING\" -lt 0 ] && ETA_REMAINING=0\n    fi\n    if [ \"$rc\" -eq 0 ]; then\n        printf \"\\r\\033[K \\033[1;32m✔\\033[0m \\033[0;37m[%s]\\033[0m %s \\033[0;37m(%s)\\033[0m\\n\" \"$counter\" \"$msg\" \"$(_fmt_secs $el)\"\n    else\n        printf \"\\r\\033[K \\033[1;31m✘\\033[0m \\033[0;37m[%s]\\033[0m %s \\033[0;37m(%s)\\033[0m\\n\" \"$counter\" \"$msg\" \"$(_fmt_secs $el)\"\n    fi\n    return {"output": "function telegram($method, $datas = [], $token = null)\n{\n    global $APIKEY;\n\n    $token = $token === null ? $APIKEY : ***\n    $url = \"https://api.telegram.org/bot\" . $token . \"/\" . $method;\n\n    foreach (['chat_id', 'from_chat_id'] as $chatIdKey) {\n        if (array_key_exists($chatIdKey, $datas) && isTelegramChatIdEmpty($datas[$chatIdKey])) {\n            return [\n                'ok' => false,\n                'error_code' => 400,\n                'description' => 'Bad Request: chat_id is empty'\n            ];\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (isset($datas['message_thread_id']) && intval($datas['message_thread_id']) <= 0) {\n        unset($datas['message_thread_id']);\n    }\n\n    $ch = curl_init($url);\n    if ($ch === false) {\n        error_log('Unable to initialise cURL for Telegram request.');\n        return [\n            'ok' => false,\n            'description' => 'Unable to initialise cURL for Telegram request.'\n        ];\n    }\n\n    curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER, true);\n    curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_TIMEOUT, 10);\n    curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_CONNECTTIMEOUT, 5);\n    curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS, $datas);\n\n    $rawResponse = curl_exec($ch);\n    if ($rawResponse === false) {\n        $curlError = curl_error($ch);\n\n        if ($curlError !== '') {\n            error_log('Telegram request failed: ' . $curlError);\n        }\n\n        return [", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}
